# Smart-map completion matrix (generated)

> Generated by `scripts/smart-map-matrix.py` from `docs/SMART_MAP_COMPLETION_MATRIX.json`. Do not edit this
> file by hand: change the JSON and run the script. `--check` fails if this file is out of date.

Checklist: `docs/IME_Full_Smart_Map_Implementation_TODO_2026-11-08.md` (330 tasks, section B). Governing plan:
`docs/IME_Recursive_Case_Map_Feasibility_and_Implementation_Plan_2026-11-08.md` (§17–18).

**How to read this.** The rows record three kinds of evidence separately: *implemented* (code at the inspected
commit), *observed* (seen in the UI, from `docs/evidence/ui-walk-2026-11-07/JOURNEYS.md`) and *measured benefit*
(human study). No overall percentage is given on purpose.

- `AUTOMATED_VERIFIED` means a named test exists that exercises the behaviour, the requirement is substantively
  met, and the test name was confirmed in `KalsmritikoshTests/`. The audit did **not** run these tests at the
  inspected commit; run times are in `docs/CASE_MAP_EXECUTION_MATRIX.md` and CI.
- `IN_PROGRESS` covers everything partly done, including work that exists only for one user on one Mac.
- `BLOCKED` is used only when the local preparation is finished and the next step needs an external account,
  a deployed service or a real person. A row that is not finished locally keeps its local status, and its
  eventual external need goes in `blocker`.
- `commit_sha` is the last commit touching the listed `implementation_paths`. It is not a verified release
  candidate; `candidate_build` stays empty until a candidate is frozen (SHIP-01).
- `needs`: a = local code only; b = server or identity infrastructure; c = real humans; d = owner accounts.

## Tasks that assume server or identity infrastructure (needs b)

| ID | Status | Requirement | What it assumes / remaining limit |
|---|---|---|---|
| ARCH-08 | BLOCKED | Choose the actual team architecture | Local preparation only: docs/SMART_MAP_M0_BASELINE.md §5 lists what the code provides (stable actor ids, separated times, atomic writes with revisions, id-addressable memberships) and the seven decisions a runnable design must make; the shared-folder sync is rejected as the transport. No design is chosen and nothing is runnable. |
| TIME-06 | IMPLEMENTED | Attribute every meaningful action | case_actions keeps the recorded name (unchanged by renames) and adds this Mac’s signed-in account (labelled not an identity check), reported performer, software actor and trigger (CaseActionContext) — sealed when present. Local part done; authenticated users need identity infrastructure (needs b). Callers do not yet set CaseActionContext for automated work. |
| TIME-08 | IMPLEMENTED | Define concurrent audit ordering | Per-ledger recording-device id, monotonic device sequence, causal parent and received-at column on case_actions; CaseAuditOrdering orders by device sequence, marks clocks running backwards and actions outside the device order. Local part done; a multi-device/server sequence and receipt service needs b. received_at is never set yet (no import path). |
| PEOPLE-05 | BLOCKED | Add stable participant identities | Local part done: participants() keeps names that recorded actions on this Mac (with the session account, labelled not an identity check), names reported as performers (CaseActionContext.performedBy) and case people apart — an external reported performer never appears as a recording user; nothing is marked authenticated. Binding authenticated accounts to case roles needs the team hosting/identity decision. |
| PEOPLE-06 | IMPLEMENTED | Expose investigator work histories | case_work_assignments (v182): assign, reassign (old ends 'reassigned to …'), complete/release, transfer on departure; workHistory lists assignments and their ends, received reassignments, recorded work, work reported as the person's, submitted results, reviews and team periods, each with the map record it opens (or why none). Local names only; multi-investigator accounts and a UI are not built. |
| PEOPLE-08 | IMPLEMENTED | Track capability and conflicts | investigator_capabilities and investigator_availability (v182, not case data; removable with the investigator's profile): declared capabilities with their basis and declared unavailability; routingCandidates lists the team alphabetically with every reason for and against (declared capability, availability, conflict check, membership) — no score; assignWork refuses an unwaived conflict and a capability the assignee has not declared. Local names only. |
| PEOPLE-09 | IMPLEMENTED | Preserve role changes and departures | case_team_memberships (v182): members join with a role and leave with a reason; open work must be transferred (in the same savepoint) to an active member; historical attribution is untouched; CaseMembershipGate is read from the ledger at the moment of acting, so an ended member cannot assign, be assigned, decide late contributions or have a queued generated result applied. No cached authorization exists to revoke (single-user v1). |
| PEOPLE-10 | BLOCKED | Respect profile access and erasure | Local part done: case_person_restrictions (v182) — a restricted profile withholds its attributes, observations, roles and statement items from personProfile and statementHistory and says how many were withheld; lifted with a reason. Person erasure now also removes the person's roles, attributes, observations, same-person assessments and restrictions, and lists interview items, late contributions and impact paths that may name them; case erasure removes every v182 case table. Per-viewer filtering of search, graph links, summaries, history and exports needs an access-control model. |
| ACTION-05 | IMPLEMENTED | Add eligible assignment | Local rule (v1, recorded per decision in case_action_routing): a kept deliberate assignment, else the designated owner if eligible, else an eligible person (team, declared capability, availability, conflict check) with the least open work then alphabetical, else in a solo case the case owner (never software); nobody eligible → unassignedQueue with reason and remedy. Assignment, engine owner and the assigned state are one write. People are names recorded on this Mac. |
| ACCESS-01 | BLOCKED | Establish authenticated identities | Local part: people are local actors (local_actors: a name and a case-folded key; identity provider/subject/bound-at columns empty and never rebound once set); a bound principal is refused with 'needs ARCH-08'; names are stated everywhere as not identity proof (help, threat model §2a). No accounts, sessions, MFA, passkeys or recovery exist. |
| ACCESS-02 | IMPLEMENTED | Bind roles per case | Per-case roles held by local actors: owner, investigator, reviewer, contributor, viewer (case_access_grants; several per person; none reaches another case; revoke-only, access generation). The first role makes the case a team case with the granting person as owner; a solo case records nothing and this Mac's owner holds every role. Responsibilities (lead, specialist, evidence coordinator) stay the S6 team memberships; roles are authorization. Not authenticated. |
| ACCESS-03 | IMPLEMENTED | Enforce authorization in services | One policy check (CaseAccessPolicy.require) inside the case file's savepoint for every change (the central write() path classifies each action: administer/review/work/contribute/export/read, unknown = work) and every other mutating entry point; requireRead for every read of case content; exports rechecked at release; findings approval needs review. An enumerating test parses the case services (Investigate/CaseFile + InvestigationCaseRepository) and fails on any unguarded change or read (exemptions listed with reasons). Not covered by the enumeration: other repositories that hold case data outside the case file (ACH, studio records, desk review, job results) keep their own case checks; field/compartment roles are not modelled. |
| ACCESS-04 | IMPLEMENTED | Separate participation from case subjects | Roles live on local actors, separate from case people (subjects, witnesses): granting or removing a role never touches case_people or their evidential roles (tested). A named person needs no actor; an actor needs no case-person row. No accounts to bind (ARCH-08). |
| ACCESS-05 | IMPLEMENTED | Scope contributor access narrowly | Contributor role: may return material (contribute) and read only the requests shared with it (scope = request ids of this case, validated); it cannot open the case, its list entry, title or counts (caseload leaves the case out). There is no external submission channel (TEAM-03), so a contributor can only act at this Mac. |
| ACCESS-06 | IMPLEMENTED | Protect metadata and derived views | Derived views read through the services check the acting person: the case list (titles, counts, due dates) leaves out cases they may not open; the change feed, map snapshot, history and every case-file read refuse; answer-maturity notices and reminders carry no case content. Within a case a viewer sees everything (no compartments per viewer); protected identities keep their output-time scrub. |
| ACCESS-07 | BLOCKED | Implement invitation and revocation | Local part: removing a role (revokeAccess) takes effect at the next read or change, including queued automation started by the person; no one grants themselves a role; the last owner stays. Invitations (links, expiry, reuse resistance) need a server and identities. |
| ACCESS-08 | IMPLEMENTED | Define offline authorization | Local offline model: grants are read from the ledger at the moment of every guarded read or change (no cached grant); queued automation runs as the person who started it and is refused after a revocation; long operations hold a CaseAccessTicket (access + erasure generation) revalidated before their effect; exports already made are not recalled (stated). No server authority to recheck queued edits from other devices. |
| ACCESS-09 | IMPLEMENTED | Enforce assignment/review authority | Administer (roles, automation policy, holds, stage/close/reopen/archive, intake policies, profile restrictions, team membership) needs the owner role; review decisions (allegations, results, points, identity decisions, findings approval) need owner or reviewer; work needs owner or investigator; no one grants themselves a role or edits a grant (append-only, trigger). Not authenticated. |
| ACCESS-11 | IMPLEMENTED | Audit access administration | Every role given or removed is an append-only grant row (who, to whom, role, shared requests, reason, generation, who removed it, why, transfer) plus a case action (accessGranted / accessRevoked) in the sealed action log; accessHistory lists it. The actor is a local name, not an authenticated identity. |
| ACCESS-12 | IMPLEMENTED | Test hostile and stale clients | Local hostile-client tests: forged actor (recorded name differs from the acting person) refused; unknown names, revoked people, contributors and viewers refused at the service; cross-case role reach refused; protected aggregates (case list) hidden; bound identities refused; stale tickets refused. Revoked tokens and cached privileges of a real backend cannot be tested (none exist). |
| TEAM-01 | BLOCKED | Deliver the real shared service | Not built by decision: real multi-user sync is to be built on Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release. Local readiness: roles, access generations, erasure generations with a propagation state and tombstones are in the ledger for a sync service to carry. Folder sync still carries one person's user layer only. |
| TEAM-02 | BLOCKED | Implement scoped contribution requests | Local part unchanged from S6/S8: requests with purpose, custodian, items, due date and states on the map; a contributor role can be limited to named requests (share scope). No delivery channel or authenticated recipient. |
| TEAM-03 | BLOCKED | Make external submission practical | Nothing local can stand in for an external portal. The contributor role and its request share are ready for it. |
| TEAM-04 | IMPLEMENTED | Reuse canonical intake | Received material goes through the case's own source versions and the intake ledger (recordSourceArrival now needs the contribute right of the person who entered it); no second store. Nothing remote arrives yet. |
| TEAM-05 | BLOCKED | Synchronize graph and work state | No team-visible store exists, so nothing local is shown as visible to the team. Folder sync shows last sync, summary and error. |
| TEAM-06 | BLOCKED | Resolve concurrent edits explicitly | Unchanged: folder-sync merges per field with the losing value listed; case records are not synchronised at all. Record-specific merges belong to the team service. |
| TEAM-07 | BLOCKED | Handle offline and interrupted transfers | Unchanged locally: folder sync keeps changes after a failed write, deduplicates intake files and holds operations until the document arrives; an erased row is never re-created. No resumable upload. |
| TEAM-08 | IMPLEMENTED | Notify without leaking | In-app notifications carry no case content: case reminders ('A case task is due today') and now answer-maturity notices ('New information about a question you asked earlier…') — no subject in the text, the stored payload or the request id. No preview preferences UI; no email or push. |
| TEAM-09 | IMPLEMENTED | Manage reassignment and departures | Removing a person's last working role with open work requires a person who can work in the case to take it over (transferred in the same savepoint, 'Transferred from … when their role was removed'); their later changes, reads and queued automation are refused; everything they recorded keeps their name. Not authenticated. |
| TEAM-10 | IMPLEMENTED | Reconcile case closure and late replies | Locally: a closed case takes no new work; a late change to an erased case is refused even when its row comes back (erasure generation); queued work rechecks access. No remote late replies (TEAM-03). |
| TEAM-11 | IMPLEMENTED | Verify team history and briefing | History names who added, performed, reviewed and changed each item (actor per action, before/after copies) and now who gave and removed each role (accessHistory). Names are local, not authenticated; no handover brief screen. |
| TEAM-12 | IMPLEMENTED | Prove both operating modes | The same work runs solo (no setup, the owner holds every role, self-review recorded) and as a team (owner, investigator/examiner, contributor) with the same records and the roles enforced. Genuine authentication and conflict handling between devices remain blocked. |
| RECOVERY-05 | NOT_STARTED | Prevent replayed external effects | No outbound-operation identity or reconciliation model exists. The app sends no letters, notifications or assignments today (the draft letter is not sent) |
| RECOVERY-08 | NOT_STARTED | Preserve collaboration boundaries | There is no multi-user identity, session or token model, so there are no collaboration boundaries to restore. Keychain items are not in backups by construction |
| SEC-04 | BLOCKED | Implement shared-data encryption boundaries | Unchanged locally: the sync folder uses AES-GCM under a passphrase key with signed batches (store.json and device names plaintext). No case record, role or erasure record is synchronised. Who can decrypt shared content, rotation and member revocation belong to the CloudKit design. |
| SEC-05 | IMPLEMENTED | Protect credentials and sessions | Now also tested: no log statement in any source file interpolates a credential-named value as public; credential-shaped text (provider keys, bearer tokens, JWS, PEM private keys, cloud and GitHub tokens) becomes '[credential removed]' in every case export and the case action log. Relay credential (StoreKit JWS) as before, verified against a test chain only; no session tokens or rotation; relay not deployed. Pattern-based masking misses unusual formats. |
| SEC-08 | IMPLEMENTED | Prevent resurrection after erasure | Erasure generations (scope hash, never a name; propagation state localOnly) and content-free tombstones written by triggers while an erasure runs; the sync engine never re-creates a tombstoned row; a late writer holding an erased case's id is refused even if the case row comes back; restores stay handled by the erasure register. Limit: a full erase empties the tombstones, so a sync folder still holding older batches can bring rows back after a full erase; tombstones are not propagated (no service). |
| SEC-12 | IMPLEMENTED | Test authorization and race failures | Local races now covered too: role removed after an export was prepared (release refused, no file, refusal recorded); a ticket taken before a revocation or an erasure is refused as stale; queued work rechecks the starting person's role; plus the earlier eight (consent change, export reauthorisation, hold during erase, late results, cross-case ids). An AI send in flight is protected by consent/scope revalidation, not yet by the role ticket. |
| PERF-09 | NOT_STARTED | Exercise service outages and backpressure | No shared case service exists: SyncCore is a folder-based replay and the relay only does AI billing. No backpressure or outage simulation found. Needs a deployed multi-user sync service. |
| PERF-12 | IN_PROGRESS | Monitor without collecting case content | On-device MetricKit diagnostics exist with no case content. The relay has only Cloudflare observability enabled. There are no health, queue-lag or error-class dashboards for a deployed service. Needs the deployed relay or sync service and an operator account. |
| QA-07 | NOT_STARTED | Run solo and distinct-user journeys | No user identity or authentication exists; the app is single-user and Sync is folder-based between devices (Kalsmritikosh/Sync/SyncEngine.swift). Assumes real accounts, a sign-in service and roles enforced on a server |
| SHIP-03 | NOT_STARTED | Complete production collaboration operations | No collaboration service exists. The relay is only for AI billing and is not deployed (database_id REPLACE_AFTER_wrangler_d1_create; AIIncludedRelay.host is empty). Assumes a hosted, scoped multi-user service with staging, production, backups and monitoring |

| ID | Status | Needs | Requirement | Automated evidence | UI evidence | Remaining limit | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CHANGE-01 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Emit typed change events | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.typedEventsAndAggregation`<br>`CaseChangeFeedTests.seenIsNotApproval` | – | Feed kinds now also cover identity decisions (and their withdrawal), person roles, assignments/reassignments, access and team changes (profile restrictions, memberships), statement corrections and contributions queued for a closed case — each read from the row its mutation wrote in the same savepoint. Every kind has a category (new material, amendment, scope, identity, relationship, assignment, review, access, work, output); every case-log action is classified semantic or cosmetic (layout, saved views, briefs); layout saves write no feed entry (tested). Limits: some feed entries are still derived at read time from durable state (document versions, readability, task status) and say so; there is no separate append-only event table. | Show categories as filters in What changed; walk it in the running app. |
| CHANGE-02 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Store derivation dependencies | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.derivationInputsExplainStaleness` | – | case_derivation_inputs (v182, append-only): bindDerivation records, for a summary, comparison, finding, output, report point, decision or brief, each input's revision and a fingerprint/snapshot (title, revision, wording digest, review, state, source versions); an input may itself be a derived item. CaseDerivation.staleness explains per input why it is stale (newer source version, changed revision/review/state/wording, input no longer in the case, input re-derived or itself stale). Limits: existing producers (brief writer, output pipeline, comparisons) do not yet call bindDerivation automatically; no inspector UI. | Bind the brief, report comparison and output producers automatically; show the explanation in the inspector. |
| CHANGE-03 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Traverse effects to relevant depth | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.typedTransitiveTraversal`<br>`SmartMapS6ChangeTests.derivationInputsExplainStaleness`<br>`CaseChangeFeedTests.sourceRevisionFlagsDependents`<br>`SmartMapS3RuleTests.cyclesDepthBreadth` | – | CaseDependencyTraversal follows dependency links only (supports/challenges/qualifies, received results, corrections and requests' questions forward; derivations, dependencies and report selections backward; recorded derivation inputs) to any depth, each record once, with the path text for every dependent, a record budget and cancellation, and counts the links it did not follow (mentions, attribution, identity pairings, money parties, time order, placements, unassessed rows). CaseChangeImpactAnalysis.impact and the map's Changes view use it; decisions are never reversed (flagged only). Tested on a 2,000-deep chain, a cycle and social adjacency. Limit: no UI walk. | Walk 'What rests on it' in the running app. |
| CHANGE-04 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Separate refresh from approval | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.refreshIsNotApprovalAndLateResultsAreFenced` | – | applyGeneratedBrief writes a generated text as a new unaccepted version (origin 'generated', labelled) beside the accepted one; CaseBriefRefreshView shows the accepted version with its stale flag and the proposal; the database refuses edits to an accepted version; a person's unaccepted draft is never covered by a generated one; only acceptBrief (a person) accepts. Limits: briefs are the only refreshable drafts; findings/report text are not regenerated; no UI for proposals yet. | Show proposals beside accepted briefs in the brief panel; extend to other draft kinds. |
| CHANGE-05 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Build a personal change review | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.personalReview`<br>`SmartMapS6ChangeTests.typedEventsAndAggregation`<br>`CaseChangeFeedTests.seenIsNotApproval` | JOURNEYS JOURNEYS #6 What changed present only | case_change_seen_people (v182): each person's own seen position (the case-level row stands in only for the person who marked it); case_change_acknowledgements: a substantive acknowledgement with a required note, append-only, deciding nothing; CaseChangeObligations lists changes needing a look (evidence with dependents, reopened reviews, detections, identity decisions, late contributions) and keeps them after the seen position passes them (markSeen first writes the durable impact notifications). Tested: marking seen clears no obligation, changes no review; Ben's position is independent of Ada's. Limits: names are local names, not accounts; no acknowledgement UI. | Add Acknowledge to What changed; bind persons to accounts when ACCESS exists. |
| CHANGE-06 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Aggregate without concealing | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.typedEventsAndAggregation` | – | CaseChangeDigest.groups: every new change grouped by category and kind, each group listing all members (complete drill-down), the number of records resting on it (typed traversal) and how many are unresolved (not acknowledged); the overview now shows every group (the prefix(6) cut is gone) and says how many changes lie beyond the display cap. Limit: the overview list is not yet a drill-down control (the full feed sheet is). | Make each group open its members in the feed; walk it in the app. |
| CHANGE-07 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Handle closed cases | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.closedCaseQueuesLateContributions`<br>`SmartMapS6ChangeTests.refreshIsNotApprovalAndLateResultsAreFenced`<br>`CaseOwnershipTests.closedCase` | – | case_late_contributions (v182): queueLateContribution (closed cases only), contributeOrQueue for background work (applies, or queues on caseClosed — nothing changes the closed case, nothing is dropped), decideLateContribution: admit only after a recorded reopen, decline with a reason (kept), or ask to reopen (the case stays closed). Decided entries are final (database guard); queued items appear in What changed; a late generated result is queued too. Limit: existing background producers (imports, job results) do not yet route through contributeOrQueue; no queue UI. | Route import/job-result writers through contributeOrQueue; add the queue to the closed-case screen. |
| CHANGE-08 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Invalidate asynchronous work | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.refreshIsNotApprovalAndLateResultsAreFenced`<br>`ClaimEngineTests.staleResaveCannotEraseReview` | – | CaseResultFence (newest brief version, case content fence, policy, requester, generation) is taken when a job starts; resultFenceIsCurrent lets a job cancel early; applyGeneratedBrief re-checks every fence inside the write and refuses a newer human edit, changed inputs, a changed policy, an ended requester or a closed case; every attempt is recorded in case_result_applications (append-only). Limit: only generated briefs go through this fence; other AI write paths keep their own revision checks. | Route other AI-applied results (drafts, proposals) through the same fence. |
| CHANGE-09 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Reconcile outages and missed events | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.reconciliationIsIdempotent`<br>`CaseChangeFeedTests.failedFeedIsNotEmpty` | – | reconcileImpacts writes one impact notification per (change, dependent) with a deterministic id (INSERT OR IGNORE) and moves a durable checkpoint (case_change_checkpoints: reconciled-through, runs) in one savepoint: an injected crash part-way writes nothing, a rerun adds each once, another adds none; acknowledged notifications are never recreated or edited. Limit: reconciliation runs when the feed is marked seen or called; there is no background scheduler. | Reconcile on case open as well; show notifications in What changed. |
| CHANGE-10 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Verify stable presentation | `SmartMapS6ChangeTests.stablePresentation`<br>`CaseChangeFeedTests.sourceRevisionFlagsDependents` | – | Before/after comparison after a source revision: every placement kept, unaffected titles, states, reviews and rich card lines identical, link ids and labels identical, the unaffected branch's fold summary identical (0 new) while the affected fold reports new items, completed/accepted work unchanged; plus the existing workspace test for positions, view and selection. Limit: service-level only; not observed in the running UI. | Observe the same comparison in the running app (UI evidence). |

Local names only. **Blocker:** Routing across a real team needs distinct authenticated participants (ARCH-08, needs b). | Add capability/availability editing and the routing list to the UI. |
| PEOPLE-09 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Preserve role changes and departures | `SmartMapS6PeopleTests.workHistoriesCapabilitiesAndDepartures`<br>`SmartMapS6ChangeTests.refreshIsNotApprovalAndLateResultsAreFenced` | – | case_team_memberships (v182): members join with a role and leave with a reason; open work must be transferred (in the same savepoint) to an active member; historical attribution is untouched; CaseMembershipGate is read from the ledger at the moment of acting, so an ended member cannot assign, be assigned, decide late contributions or have a queued generated result applied. | PEOPLE-10 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Respect profile access and erasure | `SmartMapS6PeopleTests.restrictionAndErasure`<br>`CaseErasureTests.personErase`<br>`CaseErasureTests.personEraseCoversMap` | – | Local part done: case_person_restrictions (v182) — a restricted profile withholds its attributes, observations, roles and statement items from personProfile and statementHistory and says how many were withheld; lifted with a reason. Person erasure now also removes the person's roles, attributes, observations, same-person assessments and restrictions, and lists interview items, late contributions and impact paths that may name them; case erasure removes every v182 case table. Per-viewer filtering of search, graph links, summaries, history and exports needs an access-control model. **Blocker:** Per-viewer restrictions need ACCESS identities and roles (ARCH-08, needs b). | After ACCESS: apply restrictions to search, map links, briefs and exports per viewer. |

### MONEY (M2; depends on: DATA, SRC, GRAPH, PEOPLE, TIME)

| ID | Status | Needs | Requirement | Automated evidence | UI evidence | Remaining limit | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MONEY-01 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Add canonical financial objects | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.inv104AcrossSurfaces`<br>`SmartMapS7MoneyTests.ownershipAndFlowFigures` | – | Canonical records (v183): accounts (identifier + normalised key, one per institution), holder/owner/controller assertions, statements, money records (movement, invoice, claim, reserve, balance, quotation, allegation, estimate, asset, statement row). Each account and record is one map item (moneyAccount / moneyRecord, linkable), linked to its accounts, its document and the holder person; the same record is in the people view (forPerson, account→person link), the time view (its day) and the question view (an investigator link). Limits: no entry screen yet — records are created through the service (the forensic studio does not write them, and the studio's JSON schedule stays separate; scheduleRows() feeds it); fund flow (FundsTrace) still reads the ledger's payment edges independently. | Add an entry screen for accounts, statements and money records, and let the forensic studio read and write them. |
| MONEY-02 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Reuse exact arithmetic | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.readingAndClassification`<br>`FundsTraceTests.formatting`<br>`ForensicEngagementTests.currencyAndReconciliationSafety` | – | Each record keeps the amount as written, the exact value (Decimal as text) and how it was read; FAMoney.read adds stated scales (thousand, lakh, crore, million, billion) and English number words with the basis shown ("2.5 lakh read as 2.5 × 1,00,000"); anything else stays unreadable. FundsMoneyParser keeps a negative sign (a negative-only amount is left unassigned); the JobFields amount display uses the amount's own currency precision (none stated: the stored digits). Limit: the schedule's own unstated currency is still keyed '' (labelled 'currency not stated'); Indian 1,00,000 grouping is used only in the stated basis, display groups by thousands. | Offer Indian digit grouping as a display preference. |
| MONEY-03 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Classify money mentions | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.readingAndClassification`<br>`SmartMapS7MoneyTests.inv104AcrossSurfaces` | – | Only a classified movement enters a figure; allegation and estimate kinds added (schedule and records); an unclassified record is in no figure; a classification changes only from 'unclassified' (trigger) — otherwise by amendment. Fund flow classifies amount facts before attribution: a fact whose own matched words say invoice/amount due, balance, estimate/quotation, alleged, claim or reserve is set aside as 'not a movement'. Limit: the extractor rarely keeps those words (raw_match only; the subject label is often the file name and is not used), so fund flow still treats an unlabelled single amount in a one-payment document as the payment, and says so. | Keep the words around each extracted amount so fund flow can classify it. |
| MONEY-04 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Establish transaction identity | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.identityOutcomesAndPaths`<br>`SmartMapS7MoneyTests.inv104AcrossSurfaces` | – | Rule: same reference = one payment; different references or days, or two rows of one document/statement = two; otherwise a candidate, counted once with what the figure would be if separate (ifCandidatesSeparate), until a person confirms (same) or splits (distinct) it — append-only decisions, the latest per pair counts. Every occurrence stays as provenance (INV-104: statement + byte-identical copy + report = 3 transactions from 10 records). Limit: matching is within the case's own records; the ledger's FundsTrace transactions are not yet confirm/split-able (they remain flagged only). | Offer confirm/split on fund-flow transactions too. |
| MONEY-05 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Separate financial outcomes | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.identityOutcomesAndPaths`<br>`SmartMapS7MoneyTests.inv104AcrossSurfaces` | – | Separate figures per currency — gross paid, received, returned, fees, outstanding (invoice/claim less settling movements), disputed, onward and internal — each with its contributing records (drill). Net outflow = gross + fees − returned, labelled as arithmetic, not a loss. Limit: no UI shows these figures yet (report text and service only). | Show the outcome figures in the case's money view. |
| MONEY-06 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Prevent path double counting | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.identityOutcomesAndPaths`<br>`ForensicEngagementTests.multiHopAndInternalTransfers` | – | Parties are matched through the accounts' recorded holders valid on the day (holder/joint holder; a signatory, controller or beneficial owner does not make an account's money theirs), else the account, else — flagged — the written name. Onward hops and internal transfers are shown, not added; a 'transfer between own accounts' whose recorded holders differ is flagged. Limit: the forensic studio's own schedule still matches by written name. | Let the studio schedule rows point at case accounts. |
| MONEY-07 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Reconcile statements | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.statementsRatesAndGaps`<br>`SmartMapS7MoneyTests.inv104AcrossSurfaces` | – | Statement rows are imported from the statement's table (Markdown, CSV or tab; date + debit/credit header) with their row numbers; a statement is 'reconciled' only with both balances read, nothing unresolved, every record of the account in the period on it and no difference — otherwise 'partial' or 'difference'. Records off the statement, months no statement covers and gaps between statements are listed; copies of a statement count once. Limit: only ISO days become days; other date formats stay as written (and such rows cannot be placed in a period). | Read dd/mm/yyyy dates when the statement states its date format. |
| MONEY-08 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Handle exchange rates explicitly | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.statementsRatesAndGaps`<br>`OnlineToolsTests.exchangeRates` | – | A converted figure needs a recorded rate (rate, source, date, method), is shown beside the original-currency figures and says it is partial when a currency has no rate; a bare $ is its own 'currency not stated' bucket. Limit: the ECB online tool's result is not yet saved as a recorded rate automatically — the person records it. | Let an ECB lookup result be recorded as a conversion rate with its citation. |
| MONEY-09 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Model ownership over time | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.ownershipAndFlowFigures`<br>`SmartMapS7MoneyTests.inv104AcrossSurfaces` | – | Dated, sourced assertions per account: holder, joint holder, signatory, direct/indirect owner (with 'through'), controller, beneficial owner; basis source / supplied / note (DB checks), periods checked; holds(on:) says inside/outside/not recorded. Every display says holding or controlling is not authorisation or liability. Limit: control of organisations over other organisations (not via an account) is not modelled. | Model organisation-to-organisation ownership chains if a case needs them. |
| MONEY-10 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Explain flow visuals | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.ownershipAndFlowFigures`<br>`FundsTraceTests.traceCapsByReceivedAmount` | – | FundsTrace.nodeTotals gives every party figure of the amount diagram as an exact Decimal with its edges, facts and documents; the Fund Flow view labels nodes from it (no longer from the Double drawing weight), lists 'What each party figure is made of' with the documents, marks a partial diagram, and states the width legend and the tracing allocation assumption. Limit: the view changes were built but not observed in a GUI run; the corroboration diagram's node labels show no figure. | Observe the Fund Flow drill-down in a GUI walk. |
| MONEY-11 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Generate actionable gaps | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.statementsRatesAndGaps` | JOURNEYS 4 monthly request items and partial receipt seen | proposedGaps() derives one proposal per missing statement period, per account-month of uncovered movements and per unreconciled statement (keyed by account+period, so copies count once); syncMoneyGapProposals stores each key once (UNIQUE), re-running adds nothing; accept (optionally with a case task) or dismiss needs a reason and is final. Limit: accepting does not create the evidence request itself — the person creates the task/request and names it. | Create an evidence request directly from an accepted proposal. |
| MONEY-12 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Verify representative cases | `SmartMapS7MoneyTests.inv104AcrossSurfaces`<br>`SmartMapS7MoneyTests.readingAndClassification`<br>`SupplierINV104AcceptanceTests.supplierINV104` | – | INV-104 (answer key: gross 100,000 / returned 50,000 / net 50,000, two settlements, three copy rows, invoice not a movement, no 'loss') agrees across the canonical ledger, the map's money items, the report text (CaseMoneyReport) and the forensic schedule built from it; equal payments, copies, refunds, transfers, fees, mixed currencies, lakh/words and an amendment are exercised. Limit: the money report is not yet part of the exported map package, so 'exported package' is not checked. | Include CaseMoneyReport in the case map export and compare it in the fixture test. |

### HISTORY (M2; depends on: GRAPH, TIME, ACCESS)

| ID | Status | Needs | Requirement | Automated evidence | UI evidence | Remaining limit | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HISTORY-01 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Extend existing overlap detection | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.identifierOverlap`<br>`CaseDeconflictionTests.overlapFound` | – | CaseOverlapEntity states its basis: phone/email = exact identifier; person/organisation = same name only, a candidate. Chosen identifiers (account number, reference, phone, email, other, pattern with #) are looked for in other cases' in-scope documents as whole tokens, plus structured account matches; a pattern is a candidate; results are case names and counts only (INV-104: account 8804 found in prior case 2025-17; '880' is not). Limit: collections are not searched (cases only); the text search reads evidence blocks one case at a time (not indexed). | Search selected collections too, through an index. |
| HISTORY-02 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Separate historical selection from global search | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.historySetAndReferences` | – | A per-case history set: another case, an imported collection (workspace) or a configured external source, each with a purpose and a scope (identifiers only / records / records and sources); separate from global search. There is no kind for AI memory (CHECK refuses it); an external source is not documentary until its material is imported. Per-person authorisation (who may add a case to a history set, per-viewer restrictions) needs team accounts (ACCESS / ARCH-08); on this Mac whoever records it decides. **Blocker:** Per-person authorisation (who may add a case to a history set, per-viewer restrictions) needs team accounts (ACCESS / ARCH-08); on this Mac whoever records it decides. | Bind history-set selection to team roles once ACCESS exists. |
| HISTORY-03 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Add explicit cross-case references | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.historySetAndReferences`<br>`CaseMapLinkTests.linksChecked` | – | Cross-case references pin origin case, record (linkable kinds, checked with the origin's own write check), mode (live, or snapshot pinned to one of the origin's recorded states) and purpose; scope comes from the history source. A reference holds no copy; resolve() re-reads under today's access. The same-case checks are unchanged: a map link to another case's record is still refused. Limit: references are not drawn on the review case's map (service and export only). | Show references as items on the review case's map. |
| HISTORY-04 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Compare similarities and differences | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.comparison` | – | compare() lists shared exact account identifiers and payment references, same names (candidates only), the periods each case's money records cover, kinds of money records in one or both, and the limits (scope, closed case, restricted material). It writes nothing (case logs unchanged, no identity decision). Limit: circumstances are compared only through money records and names; questions and chronology are not compared. | Compare questions and dated events too. |
| HISTORY-05 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Implement a review case | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.reviewCase` | – | startReview() makes a case a review of another (separate mother case; the origin joins its history set); findings (agrees / disagrees / incomplete / not assessable / observation) are written only in the review case and need an active review link. The origin's revision fence and case log are unchanged by the review. | Observe the review flow in a GUI walk. |
| HISTORY-06 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Preserve available-at-the-time context | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.reviewCase`<br>`CaseHistoryTests.noInventedHistory` | – | pinAsKnownAt() pins the origin's nearest recorded state at or before a moment (refused before the baseline — nothing reconstructed); originKnownAt() returns that state, the record's recorded revisions up to then and the baseline note; a record added later is 'not in the pinned recorded state'. Limit: only map states recorded by checkpoints exist; a moment between checkpoints shows the earlier one. | None beyond recording checkpoints more often. |
| HISTORY-07 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Handle later restrictions | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.reviewCase`<br>`SmartMapS7HistoryTests.identifierOverlap`<br>`SmartMapS7HistoryTests.historySetAndReferences`<br>`CaseHistoryTests.unavailableRetainedVersion` | – | Opening a recorded state now withholds versions labelled privileged today (privilegedVersions, accessNote). Cross-case reading, searching and counting skip privileged or above-confidential documents and never say how many were skipped; a withdrawn history source, an erased origin or a privileged source leaves an unavailable reference (no retained copy). Deconfliction entity counts skip such documents too. Limit: that deconfliction filter has no new test of its own (its suite passes); Per-person authorisation (who may add a case to a history set, per-viewer restrictions) needs team accounts (ACCESS / ARCH-08); on this Mac whoever records it decides. **Blocker:** Per-person authorisation (who may add a case to a history set, per-viewer restrictions) needs team accounts (ACCESS / ARCH-08); on this Mac whoever records it decides. | Add a deconfliction test with a privileged document in another case. |
| HISTORY-08 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Verify independent review and return paths | `SmartMapS7HistoryTests.reviewCase` | – | trace() takes a finding to the original record, its recorded decision, sources (re-checked) and work links; reviewsReferencing() is the way back (review case titles and finding counts only); reviewExport() holds the review's own findings with author and time, its own case log as chronology, and the trace as it reads today. Limit: the export text is not yet released through the case output gate (CaseOutputService). | Release the review export through CaseOutputService. |

The older Status menu in the case file still offers only open/in progress/blocked/cancelled. | Expose the new states with their reason field in the case UI. |

No bulk UI yet. | Add multi-select actions in the case UI. |

Superseded/cancelled actions refuse late results. | Add the result form to the action inspector. |

The automation path does the same through the outbox. | Show reopen proposals in the change panel. |

| AUTO-01 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Version case automation policies | `PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.executionRecordsVersionAndReopens`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.requestTamperDetected`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.outboxEndToEnd`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.policiesAndModes` | – | case_automation_policies: append-only versions (mode, allowed triggers and recipes, routing, independent-review requirement, budgets). CaseAutomationRecipe: versioned, typed inputs/outputs, step groups (parallel within a group, sequential across) with review checkpoints; three built-in deterministic recipes. Every outbox row records recipe and policy versions (queued and executed), input revisions and authorized effects. The existing engine (PersonaAutomationRuntimeCoordinator) is now built by AppState and runs from a real app path (a source version becoming current). | Add a policy screen; walk it in the app. |
| AUTO-02 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Implement manual/suggest/execute modes | `CaseWholeReadingTests.consentOncePerDocumentSet`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.candidateNeverBornOpen`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.failureRecoveryAndReplay`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.policiesAndModes` | – | Modes manual (queue paused), suggest (proposals only — default), execute (the policy, as an identified rule with its version, opens proposals and routes them). The mode is stored only in the policy; AI permission only in CaseReadPermissionStore; setting either leaves the other unchanged (tested both ways); automation reads permission only at execution. | Policy screen; walk it in the app. |
| AUTO-03 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Use a durable transactional outbox | `EnrichmentJobRepositoryTests.recoverStuck`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.failedOutputNoProposalNoSuccess`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.failureRecoveryAndReplay`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.outboxEndToEnd` | – | case_automation_outbox written in the same savepoint as the causing case-log row; claim with a lease → authority recheck → engine effect (task output id derived from its receipt) → link, contract, usage and done in one savepoint. States queued, running, retrying, held, done, failed, cancelled, refused, suppressed. Crashes after the claim, after the effect, and with the receipt left 'started' all recover to exactly one output (tested). | Walk it in the app; add a queue screen. |
| AUTO-04 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Make retries idempotent | `EnrichmentJobRepositoryTests.idempotentEnqueue`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.differentEventNewOutput`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.idempotentReplay`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.failureRecoveryAndReplay`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.outboxEndToEnd` | – | Engine idempotency key = outbox row; logical key = purpose + context + input revisions (not event key or policy version), unique among active and done rows: repeated delivery, a re-observed source and a new policy version return 'already done'; waiting duplicates coalesce. A failed receipt is retried under the same key (recovery: .retryFailed, attempts recorded). | Walk it in the app. |
| AUTO-05 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Prevent recursive task storms | `PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.recursionDepthLimit`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.recursionOptIn`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.recursionSuppressed`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.stormsAndBudgets` | – | Each row records causation and correlation; work caused by automation output whose inputs were already seen up its causation chain is recorded once as suppressed ('no material new condition') — 20 deliveries of the loop gave 2 rows; a changed input queues. No depth cap on manual work. | Walk it in the app. |
| AUTO-06 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Apply visible resource budgets | `CaseReadBudgetTests.ownKeyLimitOnReadCase`<br>`CaseReadBudgetTests.planToLimit`<br>`CaseReadBudgetTests.providerUsesPreCheck`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.stormsAndBudgets` | – | Per policy: new actions and compute steps per run, new actions, provider spend and external requests per policy version (case_automation_usage); a budget stop holds the row with the reason; resumeHeld continues it; budgetStatus lists use and limits; manual actions never count. AI spending outside automation stays bounded by the case-read limits. **Blocker:** Relay deployment for credit metering (provider spend is only estimated by recipe here; no automated AI recipe ships). | Show budgets on the policy screen; meter real spend once AI recipes exist (M4). |
| AUTO-07 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Debounce and scope analysis | `ContradictionReviewInPlaceTests.dismissRerunRevise`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.outboxEndToEnd` | – | Every trigger passes CaseChangeClassifier: cosmetic case actions (layout, views, briefs, export) never enqueue; triggers for the same logical work coalesce while waiting; a recipe works on its context and inputs only; a new reading of the same version triggers nothing. IncrementalUpdater itself is still untested. | Add an IncrementalUpdater debounce test. |
| AUTO-08 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED ¹ | a | Recheck authority at execution | `CaseWholeReadingTests.privilegedExcluded`<br>`JobResultWorkflowTests.exportRecheck`<br>`PJE010AutomationRuntimeTests.evidenceReferenceDenied`<br>`SmartMapS8AutomationTests.authorityAtExecution` | – | Immediately before the effect: the case exists and is open, the requester is still on the team (ledger gate), every source input is still in the case and current, no person erasure since queuing, the current policy still allows the recipe and is not manual, and a content-sending recipe needs the case's AI permission — otherwise refused with the reason (stale input, departed requester, policy change, missing AI permission and closed case each tested). Holds do not block creating work and are not checked. | Walk it in the app. |

### ACCESS (M3; depends on: ARCH, DATA)

| ID | Status | Needs | Requirement | Automated evidence | UI evidence | Remaining limit | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ACCESS-01 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Establish authenticated identities | `SmartMapS9Tests.rolesEnforced`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.hostileInput` | – | Local part: people are local actors (local_actors: a name and a case-folded key; identity provider/subject/bound-at columns empty and never rebound once set); a bound principal is refused with 'needs ARCH-08'; names are stated everywhere as not identity proof (help, threat model §2a). No accounts, sessions, MFA, passkeys or recovery exist. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Owner: decide ARCH-08 (CloudKit sharing). Then bind local actors to CloudKit identities and require them for remote roles. |
| ACCESS-02 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Bind roles per case | `SmartMapS9Tests.rolesEnforced`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.soloCase`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.bothModes` | – | Per-case roles held by local actors: owner, investigator, reviewer, contributor, viewer (case_access_grants; several per person; none reaches another case; revoke-only, access generation). The first role makes the case a team case with the granting person as owner; a solo case records nothing and this Mac's owner holds every role. Responsibilities (lead, specialist, evidence coordinator) stay the S6 team memberships; roles are authorization. Not authenticated. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | After ARCH-08: bind roles to authenticated identities; add a roles screen. |
| ACCESS-03 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Enforce authorization in services | `CaseOwnershipTests.crossCaseRefused`<br>`CaseOwnershipTests.closedCase`<br>`InvestigationCaseScopeBoundaryTests.failClosedSource`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.everyEntryPointIsGuarded`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.rolesEnforced`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.actionClassification` | – | One policy check (CaseAccessPolicy.require) inside the case file's savepoint for every change (the central write() path classifies each action: administer/review/work/contribute/export/read, unknown = work) and every other mutating entry point; requireRead for every read of case content; exports rechecked at release; findings approval needs review. An enumerating test parses the case services (Investigate/CaseFile + InvestigationCaseRepository) and fails on any unguarded change or read (exemptions listed with reasons). Not covered by the enumeration: other repositories that hold case data outside the case file (ACH, studio records, desk review, job results) keep their own case checks; field/compartment roles are not modelled. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Extend the enumeration to the remaining case-data repositories; after ARCH-08 the principal comes from the authenticated session. |
| ACCESS-04 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Separate participation from case subjects | `CaseFileTests.conflicts`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.revocationAndDeparture` | – | Roles live on local actors, separate from case people (subjects, witnesses): granting or removing a role never touches case_people or their evidential roles (tested). A named person needs no actor; an actor needs no case-person row. No accounts to bind (ARCH-08). **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Model participant = identity plus case role, linked optionally and never merged with case_people rows. |
| ACCESS-05 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Scope contributor access narrowly | `SmartMapS9Tests.readsAndDerivedViews`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.rolesEnforced`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.hostileInput` | – | Contributor role: may return material (contribute) and read only the requests shared with it (scope = request ids of this case, validated); it cannot open the case, its list entry, title or counts (caseload leaves the case out). There is no external submission channel (TEAM-03), so a contributor can only act at this Mac. **Blocker:** A shared service between people's devices is not built (scope decision: CloudKit sharing after a solo release, ARCH-08/TEAM-01; needs b). Contributor delivery also needs TEAM-03. | Define a contributor grant (request id, shared source versions) that is enforced server-side. |
| ACCESS-06 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Protect metadata and derived views | `CaseMapRecoveryTests.exportRecheck`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.readsAndDerivedViews`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.notificationsAreContentFree` | – | Derived views read through the services check the acting person: the case list (titles, counts, due dates) leaves out cases they may not open; the change feed, map snapshot, history and every case-file read refuse; answer-maturity notices and reminders carry no case content. Within a case a viewer sees everything (no compartments per viewer); protected identities keep their output-time scrub. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Pass a viewer authorization context into CaseMapProjection, the change feed and notifications. |
| ACCESS-07 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Implement invitation and revocation | `SmartMapS9Tests.revocationAndDeparture`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.revocationRaces` | – | Local part: removing a role (revokeAccess) takes effect at the next read or change, including queued automation started by the person; no one grants themselves a role; the last owner stays. Invitations (links, expiry, reuse resistance) need a server and identities. **Blocker:** Invitations need the team service and identities (ARCH-08 / CloudKit sharing; needs b). | Design invitation tokens (scope, expiry, single use, revocation list) on the chosen service. |
| ACCESS-08 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Define offline authorization | `SmartMapS9Tests.revocationRaces` | – | Local offline model: grants are read from the ledger at the moment of every guarded read or change (no cached grant); queued automation runs as the person who started it and is refused after a revocation; long operations hold a CaseAccessTicket (access + erasure generation) revalidated before their effect; exports already made are not recalled (stated). No server authority to recheck queued edits from other devices. **Blocker:** A shared service between people's devices is not built (scope decision: CloudKit sharing after a solo release, ARCH-08/TEAM-01; needs b). | Write the offline-authorization spec (cache TTL, allowed offline mutations, reconnect recheck). |
| ACCESS-09 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Enforce assignment/review authority | `SmartMapS9Tests.rolesEnforced`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.actionClassification` | – | Administer (roles, automation policy, holds, stage/close/reopen/archive, intake policies, profile restrictions, team membership) needs the owner role; review decisions (allegations, results, points, identity decisions, findings approval) need owner or reviewer; work needs owner or investigator; no one grants themselves a role or edits a grant (append-only, trigger). Not authenticated. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Add authority rules per operation, checked in CaseFileService against role assignments. |
| ACCESS-10 | IN_PROGRESS ¹ | a | Preserve separation where configured | `CaseFileTests.batches`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.rolesEnforced` | – | Action-result reviews (S8) refuse self-review when the case policy requires independence and label it otherwise; review batches refuse the same reviewer name. Findings approval now requires the reviewer/owner role in a team case, but findings runs record no builder, so a self-approval cannot be detected or labelled there yet. | Record the findings run's builder (migration) and apply the independent-review policy and the self-review label to findings approval. |
| ACCESS-11 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Audit access administration | `CaseHistoryTests.beforeAfterKept`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.revocationAndDeparture` | – | Every role given or removed is an append-only grant row (who, to whom, role, shared requests, reason, generation, who removed it, why, transfer) plus a case action (accessGranted / accessRevoked) in the sealed action log; accessHistory lists it. The actor is a local name, not an authenticated identity. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Add an access_admin_log (actor identity, scope, before/after, time) once identities exist. |
| ACCESS-12 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Test hostile and stale clients | `CaseOwnershipTests.crossCaseRefused`<br>`CloudScopeRevalidationTests.removedFromAskingCase`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.forgedActor`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.readsAndDerivedViews`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.rolesEnforced`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.revocationRaces` | – | Local hostile-client tests: forged actor (recorded name differs from the acting person) refused; unknown names, revoked people, contributors and viewers refused at the service; cross-case role reach refused; protected aggregates (case list) hidden; bound identities refused; stale tickets refused. Revoked tokens and cached privileges of a real backend cannot be tested (none exist). **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). Token tests need the backend. | Build a hostile-client test suite against the future service API. |

### TEAM (M3; depends on: ACCESS, ACTION, AUTO, IN)

| ID | Status | Needs | Requirement | Automated evidence | UI evidence | Remaining limit | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TEAM-01 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Deliver the real shared service | `SyncEngineTests.userLayerTravels`<br>`SyncEngineTests.threeDevices`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.noResurrection` | – | Not built by decision: real multi-user sync is to be built on Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release. Local readiness: roles, access generations, erasure generations with a propagation state and tombstones are in the ledger for a sync service to carry. Folder sync still carries one person's user layer only. **Blocker:** A shared service between people's devices is not built (scope decision: CloudKit sharing after a solo release, ARCH-08/TEAM-01; needs b). | Owner: confirm CloudKit sharing; then build TEAM-01 on the S9 access model. |
| TEAM-02 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Implement scoped contribution requests | `EvidenceRequestFollowThroughTests.threeMonthlyStatements`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.hostileInput` | – | Local part unchanged from S6/S8: requests with purpose, custodian, items, due date and states on the map; a contributor role can be limited to named requests (share scope). No delivery channel or authenticated recipient. **Blocker:** Needs the team service and a contributor channel (TEAM-01 / TEAM-03; ARCH-08 / CloudKit). | Add shared-input selection and a submission channel to EvidenceRequestDetails. |
| TEAM-03 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Make external submission practical | – | – | Nothing local can stand in for an external portal. The contributor role and its request share are ready for it. **Blocker:** Needs a hosted submission channel and contributor identities (ARCH-08 / CloudKit; needs b). | Choose the contributor experience (web upload or viewer app) and the receipt format. |
| TEAM-04 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Reuse canonical intake | `EvidenceRequestFollowThroughTests.threeMonthlyStatements`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.everyEntryPointIsGuarded` | – | Received material goes through the case's own source versions and the intake ledger (recordSourceArrival now needs the contribute right of the person who entered it); no second store. Nothing remote arrives yet. **Blocker:** Remote submissions need TEAM-01/TEAM-03 (CloudKit). | Route remote submissions into the same intake and evidence-request receipt path. |
| TEAM-05 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Synchronize graph and work state | `SyncEngineTests.pendingUntilDocumentArrives`<br>`SyncEngineTests.quarantine` | – | No team-visible store exists, so nothing local is shown as visible to the team. Folder sync shows last sync, summary and error. **Blocker:** A shared service between people's devices is not built (scope decision: CloudKit sharing after a solo release, ARCH-08/TEAM-01; needs b). | Add per-record sync state and extend SyncTable coverage to case tables. |
| TEAM-06 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Resolve concurrent edits explicitly | `SyncEngineTests.fieldConflict`<br>`SyncEngineTests.noteConflict`<br>`SyncEngineTests.sameNameTags` | – | Unchanged: folder-sync merges per field with the losing value listed; case records are not synchronised at all. Record-specific merges belong to the team service. **Blocker:** A shared service between people's devices is not built (scope decision: CloudKit sharing after a solo release, ARCH-08/TEAM-01; needs b). | Add record-version checks and explicit resolution for substantive case fields. |
| TEAM-07 | BLOCKED ¹ | b | Handle offline and interrupted transfers | `SyncEngineTests.failedWriteKeepsChanges`<br>`SyncEngineTests.intakeFiles`<br>`SyncEngineTests.pendingUntilDocumentArrives`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.noResurrection` | – | Unchanged locally: folder sync keeps changes after a failed write, deduplicates intake files and holds operations until the document arrives; an erased row is never re-created. No resumable upload. **Blocker:** A shared service between people's devices is not built (scope decision: CloudKit sharing after a solo release, ARCH-08/TEAM-01; needs b). | Add a submission outbox with resumable transfer and a reached/pending view. |
| TEAM-08 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Notify without leaking | `CaseErasureTests.remindersFollowErasure`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.notificationsAreContentFree` | – | In-app notifications carry no case content: case reminders ('A case task is due today') and now answer-maturity notices ('New information about a question you asked earlier…') — no subject in the text, the stored payload or the request id. No preview preferences UI; no email or push. **Blocker:** Email/push channels need a service (needs b). | Make MaturationNotifier text generic and add notification preferences. |
| TEAM-09 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Manage reassignment and departures | `SmartMapS9Tests.revocationAndDeparture` | – | Removing a person's last working role with open work requires a person who can work in the case to take it over (transferred in the same savepoint, 'Transferred from … when their role was removed'); their later changes, reads and queued automation are refused; everything they recorded keeps their name. Not authenticated. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Design a departure flow: list owned work, reassign, revoke, keep original authorship. |
| TEAM-10 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Reconcile case closure and late replies | `CaseOwnershipTests.closedCase`<br>`ChronologyExecutorTests.resultsKept`<br>`CaseMapRecoveryTests.eraseDuringLoad`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.noResurrection` | – | Locally: a closed case takes no new work; a late change to an erased case is refused even when its row comes back (erasure generation); queued work rechecks access. No remote late replies (TEAM-03). **Blocker:** Remote late replies need TEAM-03 (CloudKit). | Add a late-submission holding state tied to request id, case generation and status. |
| TEAM-11 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Verify team history and briefing | `CaseHistoryTests.beforeAfterKept`<br>`CaseChangeFeedTests.seenIsNotApproval`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.revocationAndDeparture` | – | History names who added, performed, reviewed and changed each item (actor per action, before/after copies) and now who gave and removed each role (accessHistory). Names are local, not authenticated; no handover brief screen. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). | Bind history actors to identities and add a handover view of outstanding work. |
| TEAM-12 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | b | Prove both operating modes | `SmartMapS9Tests.bothModes`<br>`SmartMapS9Tests.soloCase` | – | The same work runs solo (no setup, the owner holds every role, self-review recorded) and as a team (owner, investigator/examiner, contributor) with the same records and the roles enforced. Genuine authentication and conflict handling between devices remain blocked. **Blocker:** Real identities need the team service and identity decision (ARCH-08; planned Apple CloudKit sharing after a solo release; needs b). Real people for the run: needs c. | Once TEAM-01 and ACCESS-01 exist, script a same-case run in both modes. |

### AI (M4; depends on: SRC, SEARCH, REASON, ACTION, AUTO, ACCESS)

| ID | Status | Needs | Requirement | Automated evidence | UI evidence | Remaining limit | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AI-01 | IN_PROGRESS ¹ | a | Keep the entire core usable without AI | `CaseWholeReadingTests.offlineSearchResults`<br>`CaseWholeReadingTests.offlinePresentation`<br>`CaseContradictionFinderTests.privacy`<br>`PrivacyPostureTests.lockedProfile`<br>`CaseMapLinkTests.linksChecked` | JOURNEYS #1-#7 walk done without AI (sample mode, no key) | No single journey test that turns AI off or loses the provider mid-case and then runs manual links, summaries, comparison and actions start to finish | Add one test that runs a case with AI off: provider fails, then manual link, allegation, request and report |
| AI-02 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED | a | Reuse current provider eligibility rules | `CloudSourceGateTests.decisions`<br>`CloudSourceGateTests.registryHonoursSpecPrivacy`<br>`OnlineProviderFailureTests.sourcesRecheckedAtSend`<br>`CloudScopeRevalidationTests.providerPathTransportSpy`<br>`CaseContradictionFinderTests.privacy` | – | openai and gemini are hard-disabled (isAvailable false). No inventory test shows that every background caller (interpreter, rewriter, analyses) goes through OnlineCloudProvider | Add a source-scan guard test: no AI send outside OnlineCloudProvider or the registry |
| AI-03 | IN_PROGRESS ¹ | a | Preview and authorize actual sends | `OnlineProviderFailureTests.changeDuringConsent`<br>`CaseWholeReadingTests.consentOncePerDocumentSet`<br>`CaseWholeReadingTests.privilegedExcluded`<br>`CaseWholeReadingTests.permissionStore`<br>`CloudScopeRevalidationTests.removedFromAskingCase` | JOURNEYS #10 intro copy only. The consent sheet itself was not seen (JOURNEYS gate note) | The stored per-case permission is keyed to the document set and destination only. Replacing the own Anthropic key or changing model does not re-ask once a permission is stored | Add the key and model identity to CaseReadPermission. UI-walk the consent sheet with a key |
| AI-04 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Minimize and scope retrieval | `CaseWholeReadingTests.privilegedExcluded`<br>`CaseWholeReadingTests.tooLargeFallsBack`<br>`CaseWholeReadingTests.maskingContinues`<br>`CloudScopeRevalidationTests.removedFromAskingCase`<br>`SensitivePromptEnforcementTests.unscopedRetrievalThrowsWhenNoAccessContextPresent` | – | Exists: case scope, privilege exclusion, masking and the too-large note. Missing: any branch or role access model, so 'inaccessible branches' is enforced only as the single-user privileged/restricted subset | After ACCESS defines branch permissions, filter model context by them and add a coverage statement test |
| AI-05 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Expose constrained tools | `QueryAIParserTests.invalidFiltersDropped`<br>`QueryAIParserTests.injectionIsInertAndClamps`<br>`QueryAIParserTests.unknownSubject` | – | No typed tool surface (search, inspect, compare, propose) is exposed to the model. ### PERF (M6; depends on: none)

| ID | Status | Needs | Requirement | Automated evidence | UI evidence | Remaining limit | Next action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PERF-01 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Define named workloads and hardware | `CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.smoke`<br>`CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.fullMeasurementNormal`<br>`CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.fullMeasurementStress` | – | The normal and stress workloads are tied to build 2108ca5f on an M4 with 16 GB, Debug build, schema 173. The fixtures are generated text, so there is no OCR or page burden, no user count, no update rates and no depth profile. | Add OCR/page, depth and update-rate parameters, and rerun on the current HEAD in Release. |
| PERF-02 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Measure stages separately | `CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.fullMeasurementNormal` | – | Projection, layout, view switch, filter, selection, culling and search are timed separately, and the evidence states that SwiftUI rendering is not covered. Intake, OCR, indexing, rendering frames, AI wait, export and restore are not timed. | Add stage timers for intake/OCR/export/restore and on-screen frame timing with signposts. |
| PERF-03 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Load graph detail incrementally | `CaseMapAccessibilityTests.listPaging`<br>`CaseMapProjectionTests.lateLoadDropped`<br>`CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.smoke` | – | Drawing is limited to 400 items, the list pages at 500 and late loads are dropped. The projection still reads and builds the whole case on first use; there is no adjacency index or lazy branch loading. | Add a branch-on-demand projection with indexed adjacency and keep the reachability test. |
| PERF-04 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Cache by identity and revision | `CaseMapLinkTests.incrementalRefreshMatchesFullRead`<br>`CaseMapLinkTests.documentChangeRebuildsEntries` | – | CaseMapJournalCache is keyed by case and a digest and falls back to a full read on any change. No cache key includes permission or scope context, and invalidation after revocation, erasure or reconnection is not tested. | Add scope and permission to cache keys and test erasure and revocation invalidation. |
| PERF-05 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Keep expensive work off interaction paths | `ResourceControlTests.narrowedLaneHoldsCap`<br>`ResourceControlTests.boundedFanOut`<br>`ResourceControlTests.lanesAdapt` | – | Ingest lanes and fan-out are bounded and narrow under pressure. Nobody has checked that navigation stays responsive during a large import, and map projection/layout is not shown to run off the interaction path. | Measure main-thread hangs while importing a large archive and record the results. |
| PERF-06 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Validate responsiveness targets | `CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.fullMeasurementNormal`<br>`CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.fullMeasurementStress` | – | In the Debug test host, normal warm load is 154 ms and stress warm load is 956 ms p50 / 1124 ms p95. Nothing has been measured in the actual UI on screen, and branch-action p95 is not a named measure. | Profile a Release build with the window on screen using signposts, and record the p95 for branch actions. |
| PERF-07 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Test deep and dense cases | `CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.fullMeasurementStress`<br>`CaseMapWorkspaceTests.countsReconcile` | – | The stress case has 50,001 records, drawing is capped at 400 with the rest disclosed, and every record is reachable in 86 list pages. There are no specific fixtures for long branches, hubs or many shared appearances. | Add hub, deep-chain and shared-appearance fixtures to the harness. |
| PERF-08 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Handle memory/disk pressure | `ResourceControlTests.criticalShedsCaches`<br>`ResourceControlTests.governorFansOutChanges`<br>`ResourceControlTests.reliefRewarmsPressureShedOnly` | – | Memory pressure sheds caches and pauses background drains without losing pending work. Nothing found handles low disk, and preview and temp-file bounds and user-facing recovery choices are absent. | Add disk-space checks before writes, with recovery UI and tests. |
| PERF-09 | NOT_STARTED | b | Exercise service outages and backpressure | – | – | No shared case service exists: SyncCore is a folder-based replay and the relay only does AI billing. No backpressure or outage simulation found. Needs a deployed multi-user sync service. **Blocker:** Needs shared server infrastructure | Build only after a shared service exists; add retry/idempotency fault tests at that point. |
| PERF-10 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Measure incremental growth | `CaseMapWorkspaceTests.stableGrowth`<br>`CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.smoke`<br>`CaseMapLinkTests.incrementalRefreshMatchesFullRead` | – | Adding 100 records moved 0 existing positions, and the journal is read incrementally. The pure projection still rebuilds the whole case (1269 ms in the stress case), and there is no multi-day growth series comparing memory. | Make the projection incremental and run a multi-day growth series in the harness. |
| PERF-11 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Set truthful operational capacity | `CaseMapAccessibilityTests.listPaging`<br>`CaseMapPerfHarnessTests.smoke` | – | Tested sizes are recorded only in an evidence folder. Help says the map draws at most 400 items and discloses the rest. There is no published capacity statement for users, and no audit showing that oversized operations app-wide fail explicitly. | Publish tested capacities in help/listing and audit silent truncation points. |
| PERF-12 | IN_PROGRESS | b | Monitor without collecting case content | – | – | On-device MetricKit diagnostics exist with no case content. The relay has only Cloudflare observability enabled. There are no health, queue-lag or error-class dashboards for a deployed service. Needs the deployed relay or sync service and an operator account. **Blocker:** Needs a deployed service and operator access | Define content-free health metrics for the relay and verify them after deployment. |
