There is no start-from-a-question path and no solo/team choice. "Start with my documents" then goes through persona, hardware, folder (Continue is disabled until a folder is added), scope and done. | Add a question-first entry and an optional template/solo choice that skips the hardware and scope screens; time how long it takes to reach the first case. |
| HELP-02 | AUTOMATED_VERIFIED | a | Deliver an optional worked sample | `HelpRegistryTests.caseMapTutorialPath`<br>`HelpRegistryTests.tutorialPath`<br>`SampleArchiveModeTests.separateDatabaseFile`<br>`SampleArchiveModeTests.ephemeralFolders`<br>`SampleArchiveModeTests.flagDecides` | JOURNEYS JOURNEYS #8 menu item Replay the Case Map Tutorial present (tutorial not run) | The map tutorial steps are question, document, passage, review, request, new evidence, revised view, report. "Comparison" maps only loosely to the review step. Back/Next are manual and the sample uses a separate ledger with in-memory folders. The tutorial has never been stepped through in the UI. | Step through the replayed case map tutorial in the running sample app and record each step. |
| HELP-03 | IN_PROGRESS ¹ | a | Explain the map's visual language | `HelpRegistryTests.caseMapContextHelp`<br>`CaseMapAccessibilityTests.firstUse` | JOURNEYS JOURNEYS #6 legend present (presence only) | The legend covers kinds, link types, states, review states and keys, using the same help text as the registry. Collapsed groups (folds) and "What changed" are explained only in help topics and the first-use banner, not in the legend. Uncertainty is shown only as the status kinds. | Add collapsed group and what-changed entries to CaseMapLegend and add them to the test. |
| HELP-04 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Add contextual hover/focus help | `HelpRegistryTests.infoTopics`<br>`HelpRegistryTests.controlsExist` | – | Explanations are mostly .help() tooltips (hover). disabled(reason:) also sets the accessibility value and hint. Nothing checks that every unfamiliar control has purpose/consequence help reachable by keyboard focus, or audits jargon. | List the map and case controls, make sure each has a focus-reachable hint or inline text, and add a test that checks this from the source. |
| HELP-05 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Provide useful empty/error states | `HelpRegistryTests.emptyStateReasons`<br>`CaseMapAccessibilityTests.emptyStates`<br>`CaseMapAccessibilityTests.failedSaveKeepsWork`<br>`CaseMapProjectionTests.failedIsNotEmpty` | – | EmptyStateKind covers no files, unreadable files, outside scope, insufficient evidence, pending review and blocked export. It has no shared case for failed AI or absent access. Restricted shows only as a map state with no remedy. | Add failed-AI and access-denied states with a next action, and test the remedies. |
| HELP-06 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Complete beginner and full guides | `HelpRegistryTests.generatedDocsMatch`<br>`HelpRegistryTests.controlsExist`<br>`HelpRegistryTests.noAbsoluteClaims` | – | The manual is generated from the registry. It covers intake, sources, map, money, AI modes, privacy, export and recovery, and the test checks that every quoted control name exists. There is no team operation (the test asserts "team review" is absent), and the beginner guide and the full guide are not separate documents. | Split the manual into a short beginner path and the full manual; add team sections when team features exist. |
| HELP-07 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Preserve complete keyboard operation | `CaseMapAccessibilityTests.commandTable`<br>`CaseMapAccessibilityTests.listPathRunsTheSameCommands` | JOURNEYS JOURNEYS #1 ⌘1–⌘4; #3 ⌥⌘Y and ⌥⌘↓ with the note focused | The case map has a list path for every command and shortcuts for views, Back, details and return. The command table has no create/assign commands. Focus order is not tested, and there is no app-wide keyboard-only audit outside the map. | Add create/assign to CaseMapCommand and do a keyboard-only run of the map and case journeys. |
| HELP-08 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Verify screen-reader and text accessibility | `CaseMapAccessibilityTests.spokenText` | – | Nodes, links, counts and the view strip have VoiceOver labels and values. Dynamic Type and contrast are not checked, and no screen-reader journey (inspect source, then act) has been done. | Audit text scaling and contrast on the map views, then do a VoiceOver walk of the source-to-action journey. |
| HELP-09 | IMPLEMENTED ¹ | a | Support motion and color needs | `CaseMapAccessibilityTests.motionAndNarrowLayout`<br>`CaseMapAccessibilityTests.spokenText` | – | Reduce Motion removes animation and is wired into the drawer. States and kinds show an icon with a text label. "Nothing moves on its own" is stated in help but not asserted by a test. No check runs with colour cues removed, and screens outside the map are not covered. | Add a test that no view switch or update changes the viewport, and check legibility in greyscale. |
| HELP-10 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Keep depth discoverable | `CaseMapAccessibilityTests.commandTable` | – | The inspector has Show details and every action is available from the list. Specialist tools sit behind Settings ▸ Modules and "More tools", a separate place onboarding mentions only briefly. No test checks that the full records can be reached from the default view. | Add in-place "more" links from case items to the specialist tools and test that full records can be reached. |
| HELP-11 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Preserve source languages accurately | `AIAssistTests.translationParts`<br>`JobDeliverableTests.rules`<br>`FactLockGateTests.devanagariDigits` | – | A translation keeps every character and says it is not the original, and a translated deliverable without its original is refused. There is no transliteration model, and nothing checks that the map or reports preserve original scripts and original date/number forms. | Add fixtures in non-Latin scripts through intake, map and report, and label any transliteration. |
| HELP-12 | IN_PROGRESS | c | Observe learnability | – | JOURNEYS JOURNEYS #8 Help menu items present (help search not exercised); #10 intro copy | The agent walk saw menus and intro text only. First use, tutorial replay, narrow window and the keyboard/VoiceOver journeys were not run, and no person has done a learnability session. **Blocker:** Needs real users at the Mac. | Run the four journeys on the candidate build, with at least one person. |

| ADAPT-01 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Map every retained job result | `PersonaJobCoverageMeasuredTests.measured`<br>`CaseFileTests.reviewedRowHandsOffIntoTheCase` | – | Job results reach the map only once carried into the case (allegation evidence, chronology, report items). | ADAPT-02 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Preserve transfer identity and review | `CaseFileTests.reviewedRowHandsOffIntoTheCase`<br>`HandoffProvenanceTests.persistedRowTravels`<br>`EvidenceRequestFollowThroughTests.decisionFromMapIsTheSameRecord` | – | A carried row keeps its citations, revision digest and review state, and a duplicate carry is refused. It can go to allegations, chronology and report. Nothing found carries results into money records or into questions as distinct from allegations. | Add carry-to-money and question targets with the same dedupe rule. |
| ADAPT-03 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Keep specialized terminology contextual | `CrossPersonaTruthInvarianceTests.sameDestinationForEverySurface`<br>`CrossPersonaTruthInvarianceTests.assertabilityDecisionPersonaBlind`<br>`PersonaTerminologyResolverTests.canonicalTokenIdentityUnchanged` | – | Persona policy cannot change truth or routing (tested). However, nothing in the app calls PersonaTerminologyResolver, and the map labels are fixed English, so no persona terms are shown on the map. | Wire the resolver into the case and map labels and test that the labels change while records stay the same. |
| ADAPT-04 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Specify advertised formats | `AllDomainSyntheticMatrixTests.capabilityMatrixIsMeasured` | – | The format coverage matrix is generated and measured against fixtures. Forensic (UFDR, Teams) and financial/tabular adapters have no field contracts or tested product versions; the Cellebrite mapper only states its own version "1". | Write a field and version contract for each forensic/financial adapter, linked to its fixtures. |
| ADAPT-05 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Preserve unsupported content | `CanonicalIngestionCompletionTests.unsupportedStaysUnsupported`<br>`CaseMapProjectionTests.distinctStates`<br>`CaseSearchTests.receipt` | JOURNEYS JOURNEYS #7 report has What the app could read | Preserved-only formats keep the original's identity and hash, and the map shows a failed or unreadable state. There is no way to attach a summary supplied by the user to unread material. | Add an attached supplied-summary record for unread originals and show it on the map item. |
| ADAPT-06 | NOT_STARTED | a | Implement generic structured exchange | – | – | The only related code is a one-way export of the relationship table (CSV) and file import receipts. | ADAPT-07 | IN_PROGRESS | d | Control external enrichment | `OnlineToolsTests.client`<br>`OnlineToolsTests.sanctionsParsingAndMatching`<br>`OnlineToolsTests.registryAndCourts` | – | FX, sanctions, GLEIF/court and web-capture lookups exist and distinguish offline, declined and HTTP errors. They do not return typed candidate objects or links into the map. There are no recipes, no pagination or cost metadata, and no partial versus no-match distinction. **Blocker:** Paid provider keys and subscriptions | Define a typed adapter result (origin, scope, time, cost, status) and route candidates into review. |
| ADAPT-08 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Test real adapter failure modes | `TeamsCellebriteImportTests.teamsNonTextCounted`<br>`TeamsCellebriteImportTests.teamsArrayRoot`<br>`ContainerSafetyTests.budgetNestedContainerCeiling` | – | Missing fields, nested containers and skipped entries are tested for some adapters. There are no tests for changed versions, missing columns, bad encodings or invalid identifiers across adapters, and nothing shows a partial import cannot corrupt canonical identities. | Add a failure-mode test set for each adapter covering those cases, with identity checks. |
| ADAPT-09 | IN_PROGRESS | a | Verify every named integration claim | `AllDomainSyntheticMatrixTests.capabilityMatrixIsMeasured` | – | The measured matrix covers file formats only. There is no bounded matrix of named products and tested versions, and no parity statement for DataWalk, i2, Casefleet, Maltego or Palantir. | Add a product/version integration matrix with tested versions and explicit non-parity notes. |
